905 entries, sync-gateway-flaky-test-ticket to sync-with-develop. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **sync-gateway-flaky-test-ticket**: Use when the user wants a CBG Jira ticket filed for a flaky or intermittently failing Sync Gateway test. The user gives a Jenkins build link or a GitHub Actions run link. The ticket is titled "Flaky test: &lt;TestName&gt;" and its description has the link and the failure output of the test. Use for requests like "file a flaky test ticket for this", "open a ticket for TestFoo, it failed here: &lt;url&gt;", "make a CBG for this flake", or a pasted CI link with "flaky". Not for fixing the test, and not for backport tickets (that is sync-gateway-backport-tickets). ([couchbase/sync_gateway](https://github.com/couchbase/sync_gateway/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sync-gateway-flaky-test-ticket) · ★453)
- **sync-gateway-release-triage**: Use when preparing Jira for one or more Sync Gateway patch or minor releases - setting behavior-change labels, deciding which CBG tickets go public in the release notes (publicise-at-ga), finding test-only or flaky-test tickets, fixing issue types, or making ticket titles short enough for release notes. Use for requests like "triage the 4.1.3 tickets", "label the tickets for 3.3.9, 4.0.9 and 4.1.3", "which tickets in this filter are test-only", or a Jira filter link with fixVersions. ([couchbase/sync_gateway](https://github.com/couchbase/sync_gateway/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/sync-gateway-release-triage) · ★453)
